# symbol_addrs.txt - EU region (SCES-51607)

# Symbol addresses imported from Going Native (RC2-Going-Decompiled)
# See https://github.com/Promises/RC2-Going-Decompiled
# Additional symbols should be added by region and address range.

memcmp = 0x00115340; // libc.a (verbatim, 96B-verified)
memcpy = 0x001153d4; // libc.a (verbatim, 96B-verified)
memset = 0x00115484; // libc.a (verbatim, 96B-verified)
rename = 0x0011d3a0; // type:func - SIF-packet builder for cmd 0x80000009, sibling of func_0011D2F0. EU address MEASURED independently from the EU ROM's own hi/lo pair in sceSifInitRpc (0x11D044 lui 0x0012 / 0x11D04C addiu 0xD3A0 -> 0x0011D3A0) - NOT assumed from USA and NOT the +0x80 data-lane rule, which does not apply in this SDK region (memset/memcpy/memcmp also coincide here). Name kept as the live splat glabel so the frozen asm and this map agree. NB it is NOT libc rename - mis-record from a 28B-prologue coincidence with libc.a _rename_r, and neither SDK body is linked into the game (searched both boot ELFs, ABSENT).
s_isnan = 0x001313c8; // libm.a s_isnan.o (verbatim, whole 56B body byte-identical to the ROM, 0 relocs). NB the object's defined symbol is actually isnan - the s_isnan spelling here is the FILENAME stem and is kept only because splat and the frozen asm already use it as the glabel
e_sqrt = 0x001310c0; // libm.a __ieee754_sqrt (verbatim, full 193w-verified vs e_sqrt.o, 6 jal-relocs only)

MapCompositeThumbnailMask = 0x002964f8; // type:func CONFIRMED (signature match) - 0x8000 mask bytes select 1 byte per set bit (8 bytes per mask byte) from srcOn/srcOff into dst - byte-identical to USA (no data refs) (USA 0x296498, delta +0x60). type:func byte-drop-protected INTERIOR ALABEL - splat head-fuses the 8-byte non-terminating epilogue stump at 0x2964F0 onto this body (same as USA 0x296490), the func-form pin does NOT split it (verified, commit 0d86ac2) - real glabel needs a splat boundary override, deferred. Identity in the Ghidra plate comment.

McChdir = 0x00127f98; // NAME REFUTED - DO NOT TRUST THE NAME. Kept ONLY as a stable build/asm-label identifier - this name IS the EU INCLUDE_ASM label and the asm/eu/.../McChdir.s filename, so renaming without a resplit breaks the build. libmc RPC cmd 0x10 - writes ONLY port into req+4 and slot into req+8 of the SMALL 0x30-byte request, resLen 4. sceMcChdir is REFUTED, not merely unproven - every path-taking cmd in this family (2 McOpen, 0xd McGetDir, 0xf McMkDir) uses the LARGE 0x414 request plus a strncpy of the path, while this body copies no string at all. Sole caller SaveLoadStateMachine case 3, gated on g_mcCardInfo[card]+0x14 == 0 meaning card UNFORMATTED, success advances to state 4 which reports result code 1. TRUE IDENTITY UNKNOWN - sceMcFormat fits the shape and gate but cannot explain the structurally IDENTICAL twin cmd 0x11 at 0x128180 under the SAME gate via a SEPARATE state entry, so both cannot be Format. VERIFIED against USA 0x127f98 (delta 0) - all 52 instruction words compared, identical except 6 %lo data refs shifted +0x80 (0x141b00 to 0x141b80, 0x137e6c to 0x137eec, 0x1430c0 to 0x143140, 0x137e68 to 0x137ee8), the known EU +0x80 data lane. The cmd immediate 0x10 is NOT among the diffs, so EU uses the same command code. Do not promote a plausible name without new evidence.
McGetEntSpace = 0x00128180; // NAME REFUTED - DO NOT TRUST THE NAME. Kept ONLY as a stable build/asm-label identifier - this name IS the EU INCLUDE_ASM label and asm filename. libmc RPC cmd 0x11 - writes ONLY port into req+4 and slot into req+8 of the SMALL 0x30-byte request, resLen 4. sceMcGetEntSpace is REFUTED for the same reason as its cmd 0x10 twin - real sceMcGetEntSpace takes a dir-path string, and every path cmd here uses the 0x414 request plus strncpy, absent in this body. Sole caller SaveLoadStateMachine case 5, gated on the SAME card-UNFORMATTED condition as case 3, success advances to state 6 which reports result code 2. TRUE IDENTITY UNKNOWN - the 0x10/0x11 pairing is unexplained and sceMcFormat cannot account for both. Do not promote a plausible name without new evidence.

WaitSprDmaD9Idle = 0x002f5ec8; // type:func - CONFIRMED MECHANISM. Spins on the DMAC CHCR at a0-0x2C00 until the STR bit (0x100) clears, i.e. waits for that channel to finish. Hand-written asm, 10 words, uses $at directly. All 8 callers write the canonical DMAC triple before calling - MADR at a0-0x2BF0, QWC at a0-0x2BE0, CHCR at a0-0x2C00 with 0x100 to start - and every a0 materialisation in the tile is lui 0x10010000, so the polled register is 0x1000D400 (the scratchpad channel) at every MEASURED call site. The polled CHCR offset is BAKED INTO THE BODY, so with that fixed base this routine is channel-specific - it is D9 and only D9. Its sibling WaitSprDmaD8Idle at 0x002F2DB0 is the same 10 words with -0x3000, i.e. 0x1000D000 (D8). Direction (to- versus from-SPR) is deliberately NOT in either name - the channel number is certain from the address, the direction is not. NON-OBVIOUS CONTRACT, do not lose it in a port - the delay slot of jr $ra is lui $ra,0x70000000, so it RETURNS WITH $ra DELIBERATELY CLOBBERED TO THE SCRATCHPAD BASE and callers immediately address SPR as 0xF90($ra). A C decompilation that just returns will silently break them. NOT UNIQUE - the EU tile contains THREE byte-identical copies of this D9 body at 0x002F3934, 0x002F41FC and 0x002F5EC8, and three of the D8 body. This pin names ONE of them. The other two are deliberately left unpinned because nothing in the body distinguishes them - only their calling unit would, and that is not established here. Do not read this name as "the" D9 waiter. USA twin is 0x002F5DD0, paired by WHOLE-BODY BYTE IDENTITY (all 10 words, size 0x28) and never by address delta. It is unpinned, left for the USA map owner.
WaitSprDmaD8Idle = 0x002f2db0; // type:func - CONFIRMED MECHANISM. Sibling of WaitSprDmaD9Idle - the same 10 words of hand-written asm, differing only in the polled offset - spins on the DMAC CHCR at a0-0x3000 until the STR bit (0x100) clears. 7 callers, every one writing CHCR at a0-0x3000 with the start bit immediately before the call, and every a0 materialisation in this call family is lui 0x10010000, putting the polled register at 0x1000D000 (DMAC channel D8, the other scratchpad channel). Carries the same NON-OBVIOUS CONTRACT as its sibling - the delay slot of jr $ra is lui $ra,0x70000000, so it returns with $ra deliberately clobbered to the scratchpad base for the caller to address SPR through. NOT UNIQUE - the EU tile contains THREE byte-identical copies of this D8 body at 0x002F2DB0, 0x002F395C and 0x002F4224. This pin names ONE of them and the other two are deliberately left unpinned, since nothing in the body distinguishes them. USA twin NOT yet located and NOT claimed. The name gives the channel number, which is certain from the address, and withholds the to- versus from-SPR direction, which is not.
BlendPackedVec4 = 0x002845f8; // type:func - CONFIRMED MECHANISM. VU0 macro-mode linear blend of two PACKED 4-byte vectors by a scalar phase. a0 and a1 are unpacked byte-to-halfword-to-word via pextlb then pextlh, moved to vf2 and vf3 and converted with vitof0. The phase arrives as a FLOAT IN $f12 (mfc1 into vf1) and vsubx.w forms 1-t in the w lane, so vmulaw ACC = A*(1-t) and vmaddx vf1 = ACC + B*t - a lerp - then vftoi0 and ppach then ppacb repack to four bytes in v0. Register a2 is never read by the body, which is why the repo declares this callee (void*,void*,void*) at the pure register-passthrough forwarder - that form is DELIBERATE and correct, since a forwarder that touches nothing leaves $f12 intact. Do not "fix" it to a float parameter. Independently agrees with the prototype note already recorded at src/usa/text/1A8180.c line 164, blend vec4 from src by phase t. USA twin is 0x002846E8, paired by WHOLE-BODY WORD IDENTITY - all 18 words, size 0x48, zero data relocs so the comparison is exact - and never by address delta. It is unpinned, left for the USA map owner.
SplitFrameCountToMinSecHundredths = 0x002ee490; // type:func CONFIRMED - splits a frame counter (arg0) into minutes, seconds and hundredths, storing each through the three out-pointers arg1, arg2 and arg3. Uses the tile int-to-float and float-to-int-truncate primitives func_002845A0 and func_002845B0 throughout. EU 50 Hz arithmetic - minutes by multiplying 0x39AEC33E (1 over 3000) then subtracting minutes times 3000.0 (0x453B8000), seconds by multiplying 0x3CA3D70A (1 over 50) then subtracting seconds times 50.0 (0x42480000), hundredths from the 0 to 49 frame remainder doubled. The final field is forced to 0 when it exceeds 0x63 (99) via slt plus movn.
